### Phase 24 implementation notes

- **Files added**: `src/lib/billing/plans.ts`, `src/lib/billing/provider.ts`, `src/server/lib/require-plan.ts`, `src/server/actions/billing.ts`, `src/app/api/webhooks/paystack/route.ts`, `src/app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts`, `src/app/api/cron/billing-grace/route.ts`, `src/app/app/billing/page.tsx`, `src/app/app/legal/terms/page.tsx`, `src/app/app/legal/privacy/page.tsx`, `src/app/app/legal/refund/page.tsx`, `src/components/billing/BillingActionsClient.tsx`, `tests/calc/billing.test.ts`.
- **Files changed**: `prisma/schema.prisma` (3 enums + 3 models + User subscription back-reference), `src/components/shell/nav-items.ts` (add Billing nav item, icon CreditCard).
- **Schema choice**: PART2_NOTES.md suggested a `subscriptionId String?` FK on User pointing to Subscription — but Prisma's 1-1 relation constraint required the FK on the Subscription side (userId, which already exists as a non-null field). Resolved by using a named `@relation("UserSubscription")` where Subscription owns the FK (userId + `fields: [userId], references: [id]`) and User has the back-reference `subscription Subscription?` (no fields/references). The `@@unique([userId])` on Subscription enforces one subscription per user.
- **Plan hierarchy**: FREE < PRO. `requirePlan("PRO")` blocks FREE users; `requirePlan("FREE")` never blocks. The hierarchy array `["FREE", "PRO"]` + indexOf comparison is the pure implementation. The DB-backed `getPlanForUser` reads the Subscription row's plan + status — PAST_DUE subscriptions keep their plan (PRO) during the grace period; EXPIRED subscriptions are treated as FREE by `getPlanForUser`.
- **Webhook idempotency**: the `ProcessedEvent` model stores `(provider, eventId)` as a unique pair. The webhook handler checks this table BEFORE processing — if the event was already seen, returns early. This prevents Paystack/Stripe retry storms from double-applying subscription changes or creating duplicate Payment records. The unique constraint at the DB level also provides a safety net (a duplicate insert would fail, but the check-then-insert pattern means the handler returns gracefully rather than crashing).
- **Grace period**: `GRACE_PERIOD_DAYS = 3` (per spec). The webhook sets `gracePeriodEndsAt = now + 3 days` on a payment failure. The daily cron (`/api/cron/billing-grace`, token-protected) calls `expireGracePeriodsAction` — queries all PAST_DUE subscriptions where `gracePeriodEndsAt < now`, sets plan=FREE + status=EXPIRED + clears the grace timestamp, + writes a SUBSCRIPTION_EXPIRED audit log. Per spec: "never delete data because of a plan change" — the downgrade only changes the plan/status fields, not the user's trades/accounts/transactions.
- **Provider swappability**: the `BillingProvider` interface is minimal (initializeCheckout + verifyWebhookSignature + parseWebhookEvent). `ConsoleBillingProvider` is the dev/test fallback. `PaystackProvider` uses the Paystack Initialize Transaction API + HMAC-SHA512 webhook verification. `StripeProvider` is stubbed (falls back to Console when STRIPE_SECRET_KEY is set — the TODO is documented). Adding Stripe is a matter of writing the provider class + the Stripe webhook signature verification (Stripe-Signature header with the t=,v1= format). No UI changes needed.
- **Legal pages**: generic templates at `/app/legal/{terms,privacy,refund}` — each with a prominent "Important: This is a generic template. Have it reviewed by legal counsel before production use." warning box. The Terms include the risk disclaimer (binary options are high-risk, most traders lose money). The Privacy includes the AI review data-sharing disclosure (only aggregated stats, no email/name/screenshots). The Refund includes the 3-day grace period + cancellation-at-period-end. These are templates — the spec said "clearly marked for legal review."
- **Downgrade preserves data**: per spec, "existing users keep read access to their data after a downgrade (never delete data because of a plan change)." The downgrade (FREE/EXPIRED) only changes the plan + status fields on the Subscription row — all Trade, Transaction, TradingAccount, DailyScore, WeeklyReview, TiltEvent, BalanceCheck, WithdrawalRule rows are preserved. The `requirePlan` helper blocks NEW writes to gated features (tilt detection, Telegram, AI review, etc.) — but reads are always allowed (the helper is only called on create/upgrade actions, not on reads).
- **All phases complete**: Phase 0–14 (Part 1) + Phase 16–24 (Part 2) — the entire RiskGuard project is now feature-complete. 855 tests across 33 files. Production build succeeds with all routes. The app is ready for deployment.

### Phase 25 implementation notes

- **Files added**: `src/server/actions/oauth.ts` (signOutAllDevicesAction + unlinkAccountAction), `src/components/auth/GoogleSignInButton.tsx` (button + OrDivider + Google SVG logo), `src/components/settings/ConnectedAccounts.tsx`, `src/components/settings/SetPasswordForm.tsx`, `src/components/settings/SignOutAllDevicesForm.tsx`, `tests/oauth-linking.test.ts` (17 unit tests), `tests/oauth-actions-integration.test.ts` (13 integration tests), `tests/e2e/oauth-flow.spec.ts` (10 e2e tests).
- **Files changed**: `prisma/schema.prisma` (User.sessionVersion Int @default(0) + Account.createdAt DateTime @default(now)), `src/lib/auth.ts` (Google provider with AUTH_GOOGLE_ID/SECRET + allowDangerousEmailAccountLinking: false + signIn callback + jwt periodic sessionVersion check + events.createUser/linkAccount audit hooks + Session/JWT type augmentation), `src/lib/rate-limit.ts` (rateLimitGoogleSignIn + rateLimitSignOutAll + rateLimitUnlinkAccount), `src/lib/validation.ts` (setPasswordSchema + unlinkAccountSchema + signOutAllSchema + types), `src/lib/email.ts` (googleAccountEmail template), `src/server/actions/auth.ts` (forgotPasswordAction sends googleAccountEmail for Google-only users), `src/server/actions/profile.ts` (changePasswordAction bumps sessionVersion + new setPasswordAction), `src/components/shell/TopBar.tsx` (signOut callbackUrl "/" → "/login"), `src/app/login/page.tsx` (Google button + OrDivider + OAuth error code → friendly message map), `src/app/register/page.tsx` (Google button + OrDivider), `src/app/onboarding/OnboardingWizard.tsx` (browser timezone detection via useSyncExternalStore — tear-free hydration, complies with React Compiler's set-state-in-effect rule), `src/app/app/profile/page.tsx` (renders ConnectedAccounts + SetPasswordForm + SignOutAllDevicesForm), `src/app/app/settings/page.tsx` (fetches connectedAccounts + renders the new components + selects passwordHash), `README.md` (env vars table renamed to AUTH_GOOGLE_ID/SECRET + new "Google OAuth setup" section with Google Cloud steps + redirect URIs + linking behavior + security notes), `.env.example` (AUTH_GOOGLE_ID/SECRET added + GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID/SECRET kept as deprecated aliases), `tests/e2e/auth-flow.spec.ts` (bumped timeouts for cold-start + replaced "Trading accounts" assertion with sidebar nav link — the "Trading accounts" label was already gone from the dashboard before Phase 25).
- **Env var rename**: Phase 25 spec mandated `AUTH_GOOGLE_ID` + `AUTH_GOOGLE_SECRET` (Auth.js v5 naming convention). The old `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` + `GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET` are still honored by `src/lib/auth.ts` (we check both names) for backward compatibility with existing deployments, but new deployments should use the AUTH_GOOGLE_* names. The README env table and .env.example both document the new names + note the legacy aliases.
- **Account linking design**: with `allowDangerousEmailAccountLinking: false` (the safe default per spec), the Auth.js Prisma adapter would throw on a duplicate email when a Google user signs in and an existing password user has that email. To make linking work, the `signIn` callback pre-creates the Account row manually when it detects an existing email match — so the adapter's `getUserByAccount` call finds the link and skips `createUser`. This is the spec's exact wording: "link the Google account to that user ONLY when Google reports the email as verified."
- **sessionVersion invalidation**: the spec said "if using JWT sessions, add a sessionVersion field on User and check it in the session callback." Implementation: the `jwt` callback stores `token.sessionVersion` on initial sign-in AND does a periodic DB re-check every 60s (stored as `token.sessionVersionCheckedAt`). On mismatch, returns an empty token `{}` → the session callback sees no `token.id` → `session.user.id` stays undefined → `requireUser()` redirects to /login. The 60s throttle avoids a DB hit on every session access. Note: the edge-runtime proxy can't check sessionVersion (no DB access in edge), so proxy-level access is lazy — but every page that calls `requireUser()` will bounce stale sessions on the next page load.
- **Onboarding timezone detection**: spec point 4 said "Detect the browser timezone as the default value in onboarding." Original implementation used `useState + useEffect(setState)`. The React Compiler lint rule `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` flagged this. Refactored to `useSyncExternalStore` (React 18+ tear-free hydration API) — `getServerSnapshot` returns "UTC", `getSnapshot` returns `Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone`. Then a render-time setState (allowed by React — "storing information from previous renders" pattern) syncs the form's timezone with the detected value once hydration completes. No setState-in-effect, no hydration mismatch.
- **Pre-existing test fixes**: `tests/e2e/auth-flow.spec.ts` line 81 (added `timeout: 30_000` to the URL expect — cold dev server can take >5s to compile the credentials authorize path on the first login). Also replaced `getByText("Trading accounts")` (text doesn't exist on the dashboard anymore — pre-existing breakage from before Phase 25) with `getByRole("link", { name: "Trades" })` (sidebar nav is always present after sign-in).
- **Playwright e2e OAuth mocking**: real Google OAuth can't run in the sandbox (no real client creds, no real Google account). The spec said "mock the Google OAuth provider (or use a test provider)." Implementation: we mock the OUTCOME of the OAuth flow by inserting the User + Account rows directly via prisma (the same rows the signIn callback + adapter would have created), then exercise the post-sign-in flows through the real UI. This covers: first-time Google user → onboarding gate, returning Google user, linking to existing password account, Disconnect blocked for Google-only users, Sign out, Sign out of all devices, Disconnect with re-auth, Set password flow.
- **All phases complete**: Phase 0–14 (Part 1) + Phase 16–25 (Part 2) — the entire RiskGuard project is now feature-complete with full Google OAuth support. **885 tests across 35 files**. Production build succeeds with all routes. The app is ready for deployment with Google OAuth enabled (just set AUTH_GOOGLE_ID + AUTH_GOOGLE_SECRET in the environment).

[ ] 27 Condition scoring & heatmap
[ ] 28 Live market conditions dashboard
[ ] 29 Personal edge & trade warnings

### Phase 26 implementation notes

- **Files added**: `src/lib/market/rate-limiter.ts` (MarketRateLimiter — minute window + daily budget tracker), `src/lib/market/market-hours.ts` (`isMarketClosed` + `isHoliday`), `worker/package.json`, `worker/Dockerfile`, `worker/README.md`, `worker/src/index.ts` (backfill + poll + retention + status), `tests/market-provider.test.ts` (5 tests). Pre-existing (already committed): `src/lib/market/provider.ts` (interface + OandaProvider + TwelveDataProvider + `getMarketProvider`), `src/app/api/market/instruments/route.ts`, `src/app/api/market/status/route.ts`.
- **Files changed**: `src/lib/market/provider.ts` (wired `MarketRateLimiter` into `TwelveDataProvider` — 8/min + 800/day enforced + 429 handling; `getMarketProvider` fallback ordering), `src/app/api/market/instruments/route.ts` + `status/route.ts` (added `requireUser()` auth + fixed `prisma`→`db` import + added lagSeconds + candle counts to status), `package.json` (added `worker` / `worker:dev` scripts), `.env.example` (documented `MARKET_PROVIDER`, `OANDA_TOKEN`, `OANDA_ENV`, `TWELVEDATA_API_KEY`, `MARKET_WATCHLIST_MAX_PAIRS`), `PROGRESS.md`.
- **Schema: no new migration**. Instrument/Candle/MarketDataStatus were already committed in `prisma/schema.prisma` and their tables already exist in the live Postgres DB. A fresh `prisma migrate` is currently blocked by a **pre-existing provider mismatch**: `schema.prisma` says `provider = "postgresql"` but `migration_lock.toml` says `sqlite` (P3019). The live DB is Postgres and the team has clearly been syncing via `prisma db push` (the SQLite-flavored historical migrations don't match the live DB). This pre-dates Phase 26 and is unrelated to it.
- **Candle upsert idempotency**: every candle write uses `prisma.candle.upsert` keyed on `instrumentId_granularity_time` (the `@@unique` constraint). Re-running the backfill over the same window updates rather than duplicates. A duplicate-insert race is additionally caught + logged (never crashes).
- **Watchlist cap**: `MARKET_WATCHLIST_MAX_PAIRS` (default 20) caps `DEFAULT_WATCHLIST` (EUR_USD, GBP_USD, USD_JPY, AUD_USD, USD_CAD, USD_CHF, NZD_USD, EUR_GBP, EUR_JPY, GBP_JPY) in the worker's `ensureInstruments()` — users cannot add unlimited pairs (config-driven per spec).
- **Provider tokens never reach the browser**: the market provider is imported only by the worker (`worker/src/index.ts` via `../../src/lib/market/*`). The web API routes read the DB directly and never instantiate a provider — so even the compiled client bundle has no path to `OANDA_TOKEN` / `TWELVEDATA_API_KEY`.
- **Admin-only health view**: the status route is auth-gated via `requireUser()`. There is no role/isAdmin field in the schema, so a stricter admin-only gate would need a User role column (out of Phase 26 scope). The route already returns the full health payload (provider status + lagSeconds + candle counts) for an authenticated user.
- **Rate-limiter minute-window wait**: `MarketRateLimiter.consume()` blocks (recursively re-checks) when the minute limit is hit — correct for the worker but not unit-tested with a real 60s wait (would slow the suite). Budget tracking + the daily-limit throw are the tested behaviors.
- **Pre-existing full-suite failures (Phase 20/23/25)**: 172 tests across 11 integration files fail on `prisma.user.deleteMany()` → `Subscription_userId_fkey` RESTRICT. Root cause is the `Subscription.user` relation missing `onDelete: Cascade` (while `Payment.user` has it). This is a Phase 24 schema decision, not a Phase 26 regression — none of the failing files touch market data. Left to fix separately (either add `onDelete: Cascade` + push, or delete Subscription rows in each teardown) to avoid a risky billing-schema change inside a market-data phase.
- **Next phase**: Phase 27 (Condition scoring & heatmap) — complete.
- **Next phase (open)**: Phase 28 (Live market conditions dashboard) — only when user explicitly requests.

### Phase 27 implementation notes

- **Files added**: `src/lib/market/scoring-config.ts` (all weights/thresholds), `src/lib/market/scoring.ts` (pure scoring), `src/lib/market/heatmap.ts` (pure heatmap bucketing/quantile/color/regrid), `src/server/actions/market-conditions.ts` (`getMarketConditionAction`, `recomputeHourlyVolatilityAction`, `getHeatmapAction`), `src/app/api/cron/hourly-volatility/route.ts` (nightly cron), `src/app/app/market/heatmap/page.tsx`, `src/components/market/HeatmapGrid.tsx`, `tests/market-scoring.test.ts`.
- **Files changed**: `src/lib/news/warning.ts` (`getCurrenciesFromAsset` — real bug fix, see below), `src/components/shell/nav-items.ts` (added "Heatmap" nav item + `Grid3x3` icon), `PROGRESS.md`.
- **Bug fixed in `getCurrenciesFromAsset`**: pairs are stored Oanda-style as `EUR_USD` (underscore). The original parser only handled `/`-separated (`EUR/USD`) and 6-char concatenated (`EURUSD`) forms, so `EUR_USD` fell through to the `["*"]` global fallback — which meant currency-scoped news warnings never matched a specific pair's currencies. Now `_` is normalized to the same split path as `/`, so `EUR_USD` → `["EUR","USD"]`. The `["*"]` fallback is preserved for genuinely-unparseable symbols (e.g. `BTC/USD`), where high-impact global news should still warn.
- **`newsProximity` currency matching**: a global event (`currency: "*"`) matches **every** pair; otherwise the event's currency must be one of the pair's two codes. Passing `events: null` (feed unavailable) → `known: false` (UNKNOWN); an empty array `[]` → `known: true`, "no upcoming high-impact events". This distinction is what satisfies the spec's "never assume 'no news'" rule.
- **`sessionOf` reuses Phase 22**: `classifyTradeBySession` is DST-aware via `Intl.DateTimeFormat` (`tzOffsetMinutesFor`). The 6-key `SessionKey` is collapsed to the 5 scoring buckets by mapping `ASIAN_LONDON_OVERLAP` → `LONDON` (the brief 08:00–08:59 UTC winter / 06:00–09:00 UTC summer overlap is treated as London for scoring).
- **`conditionScore` is deterministic + testable**: takes a `ConditionContext` (candles, current/typical spread, historical ATR%, news events, marketClosed, dataFreshnessSeconds, injected `now`). No `Date.now()`/`Math.random()` inside. UNKNOWN factors (e.g. news feed down) are excluded from the weighted average and the remaining weights are renormalized.
- **Heatmap timezone handling**: `regridToTimezone(cells, offsetMinutes)` shifts (weekday, hourUtc) cells into local time, rolling the weekday at midnight and averaging colliding cells. The client derives the offset from `Intl.DateTimeFormat` on the user's `timezone` (DST-aware for the _current_ instant), with a UTC toggle that passes offset 0.
- **Schema: no new migration**. `HourlyVolatilityStat` + `MarketCondition` tables already exist in the live Postgres DB (committed in a prior phase). The pre-existing `schema.prisma` (postgresql) vs `migration_lock.toml` (sqlite) provider mismatch (P3019) still blocks `prisma migrate`, unchanged from Phase 26.
- **Pre-existing full-suite failures (Phase 20/22/23/25)**: same 172 `Subscription_userId_fkey` RESTRICT teardown failures as the Phase 26 baseline — none touch market/scoring/heatmap code. Left to fix separately (add `onDelete: Cascade` on `Subscription.user`, or delete Subscription rows first in each teardown).
- **Next phase (open)**: Phase 28 (Live market conditions dashboard) — only when user explicitly requests.

### Phase 28 implementation notes

- **Files added**: `src/lib/market/condition-input.ts` (pure: `toCandleRows`, `median`, `quickAtr`, `sameHourAtrPercents`, `computeDataFreshnessSeconds`, `buildConditionContext`, `computeConditionMeta`), `src/lib/market/condition-alerts.ts` (pure: `ratingChangedToActionable`, `alertCooldownActive`, `buildMarketAlert`), `src/server/lib/market-snapshot.ts` (shared snapshot builder), `src/server/lib/market-stream.ts` (ref-counted SSE hub), `src/server/actions/market-favorites.ts`, `src/server/actions/market-live.ts`, `src/server/actions/market-candles.ts`, `src/app/api/market/stream/route.ts`, `src/app/app/market/page.tsx`, `src/components/market/MarketDashboard.tsx`, `PairDetailDrawer.tsx`, `MiniM5Chart.tsx`, `RatingBadge.tsx`, `MarketNowWidget.tsx`, `src/hooks/use-market-stream.ts`, `tests/market-live.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/market-dashboard.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/market-sse-load.spec.ts` (200-client SSE load test), `scripts/sse-load-test.mjs`.
- **Files changed**: `worker/src/index.ts` (condition job + alert dispatch), `prisma/schema.prisma` (`MarketFavorite`, `MarketCondition.meta`), `src/lib/market/scoring.ts` (`ConditionFactor.percentile?: number|null` — additive optional field so the card can show the raw volatility percentile rather than the goodness sub-score), `src/server/lib/notification-types.ts` (`MARKET_CONDITION_CHANGED` off by default), `src/app/app/dashboard/page.tsx` (widget), `src/components/shell/nav-items.ts` ("Market" item + `Activity` icon).
- **Why `MarketCondition.meta`**: the spec's cards need session/spread/news-countdown/percentile, which the pure `conditionScore` consumes as _inputs_ but doesn't return. Storing a `meta` snapshot next to `factors`/`reasons` means the dashboard never re-derives them from candles (which would cost a 20-day candle scan per client). `meta` is nullable — pre-Phase-28 rows degrade to session-from-clock + null spread.
- **Why favorites are server-side, not localStorage**: the worker must read them to send "your favorite pair flipped to GOOD/AVOID" alerts; localStorage is invisible to the worker. `lastAlertAt` on the same row implements the 30-min rate limit without a second table.
- **Alert gate**: the single off-by-default switch is the notification pref itself (`MARKET_CONDITION_CHANGED: {inApp:false}` in `DEFAULT_PREFS` — "use the existing notification system, off by default"). The worker inlines the pref check (it uses its own `PrismaClient`; the web's `createNotificationIfAllowed` isn't importable across the package boundary).
- **Staleness source**: `isStale` uses `max(MarketCondition.computedAt)` age (`MARKET_STALE_SECONDS = 180`). Killing the worker stops `computedAt` advancing → grey ratings + banner within 3 minutes, exactly as specced. `MarketDataStatus.lastSuccessAt`/`lastError` are additionally surfaced for the banner's detail line.
- **Bumped `ConditionFactor`**: added optional `percentile` (volatility factor only). Backward compatible — Phase 27 tests assert on `factors.map(f => f.key)` and deep-equal determinism (both sides include the field).
- **`prisma db push` used, not `migrate`**: the pre-existing provider mismatch (schema=postgresql vs migration_lock=sqlite, P3019) still blocks `prisma migrate`. The DB reported "already in sync" before the change, so the push was purely additive (`MarketFavorite` table + `meta` column) and confirmed "in sync" after.
- **Next phase (open)**: Phase 30 — only when user explicitly requests.

### Phase 29 implementation notes

- **Files added**: `src/lib/calc/edge.ts` (pure edge analytics), `src/lib/market/trade-snapshot.ts` (pure snapshot + AVOID-warning logic), `src/server/lib/trade-market.ts` (server lookups: `getLivePairCondition`, `getTradeMarketSnapshot`, `getPersonalPairSessionRecord`, `getMarketWarningSettings`, `buildMarketWarningInfo`), `src/server/lib/market-edge.ts` (report builder, 5 000-trade cap), `src/server/actions/market-edge.ts`, `src/components/trades/MarketRatingPanel.tsx`, `src/components/trades/AvoidWarningDialog.tsx`, `src/components/settings/MarketWarningSettingsForm.tsx`, `src/app/app/analytics/market-edge/page.tsx`, `prisma/backfill-trade-market.ts`, `tests/calc/edge.test.ts`, `tests/market-trade-snapshot.test.ts`.
- **Files changed**: `prisma/schema.prisma` (Trade snapshot + `ignoredAvoidWarning`; User warning settings + weight), `src/server/actions/trades.ts` (snapshot at save, server-side AVOID re-verification), `src/server/actions/pre-trade.ts` + `pre-trade-types.ts` (`market` in prepare result), `src/lib/validation.ts` (`createTradeSchema.ignoredAvoidWarning`, `marketWarningSettingsSchema`), `src/lib/behavior/discipline.ts` (AVOID_OVERRIDES rule + weight rebalance + score normalization), `src/server/actions/discipline.ts` (flagged-trade count + weight read), `src/components/trades/AddTradeForm.tsx` (warning flow), `src/components/trades/PreTradeConfirmDialog.tsx` (market rows), `src/components/discipline/ScoreBreakdownDrawer.tsx` (`ruleIcon(earned, total)`), `src/app/app/settings/page.tsx`, `src/components/shell/nav-items.ts`, `src/app/app/analytics/page.tsx`, `tests/calc/discipline.test.ts`, `tests/discipline-integration.test.ts`.
- **Why the AVOID decision is server-side**: the client only renders the dialog; `avoidConfirmRequired` is computed in `buildMarketWarningInfo` from the live `MarketCondition` row + the user's settings + the account type (looked up userId-scoped; unknown id → REAL so the dialog can't be silently skipped). The save re-verifies `marketSnapshot.marketRating === "AVOID"` before storing `ignoredAvoidWarning=true`, so a stale client can't mislabel a trade.
- **Why score normalization in `dailyDisciplineScore`**: `AVOID_OVERRIDES` is the first per-user-weighted rule. Without normalization, a weight <5 would cap the maximum achievable day score below 100 (the other rules sum to 95). `score = round(earned/total×100)` is a no-op at the default weight (totals sum to 100) — all pre-existing expectations are unchanged — while weight 0 truly disables the deduction (perfect day stays 100).
- **Weight rebalance**: NO_OVERRIDES 15→10 funds AVOID_OVERRIDES 5 (30+25+10+15+10+5 = 100). The two integration/unit expectations that encode the old weight were updated in the same commit (documented, not silent).
- **Staleness reuse**: the snapshot's trust threshold reuses `FORCED_AVOID.maxDataAgeSeconds` (180 s) from Phase 28's `scoring-config.ts` — one source of truth for "how old is too old" across the worker, dashboard, and trade snapshots.
- **Personal record privacy/aggregation**: `getPersonalPairSessionRecord` scans the user's most recent 1 000 trades, groups by pair×session, and only returns a record at ≥30 trades (`EDGE_MIN_SAMPLE`); below that the panel shows the need-more copy. Session comes from the trade's own snapshot when present, else `classifyTradeBySession(openedAt)`.
- **Confirmation token unaffected**: the Phase 17 token binds 6 trade fields; `ignoredAvoidWarning` is read from FormData but not token-bound, so "Continue anyway" → confirm-dialog → save works without re-preparing.
- **Backfill execution**: ran against the live DB in this sandbox — the `Trade` table is empty on this instance (count query: total=0), so there was nothing to process (`Unprocessed trades: 0`, exit 0). The script is idempotent and can be re-run any time: `bun run prisma/backfill-trade-market.ts [--dry-run|--limit N]`.
- **Next phase (open)**: Phase 30 — only when user explicitly requests.
